## Pass 15 owner notes — eight slices, 2026-09-30 to 2026-10-01

Ten notes from `~/Downloads/ankiscape notes.rtfd` (Pass 15, 2026-09-15 to 2026-09-30); rulings `design-thread-queue.md` §4.28, brief `codex-briefs/pass15-owner-notes-handoff-2026-09-30.md`. Opus 5.5 authored every slice at the owner's request; Kimi K3 reviewed five diffs and GPT-6.1 Sol the other three after Kimi's 5-hour limit (`403 You've reached your 5-hour usage limit`). Integration branch `goal/pass15-integration`.

- Finisher notice (`goal/pass15-finisher-notice`, 54196a1d, 416c2a86): "Wear Spiked gauntlets to finish" on its own amber HUD line; one event-log line per encounter, marked on the encounter record after the review found a missed warning on a new spawn and a repeat on Gargoyle regeneration.
- Bank trip target switch (ccba1926, e830d5b4, 01457f45): a target switch cancels a pending trip for another target.
- Fishing guide levels (a526d1b1): one guide row per fish at its own level; slimy eel 28; 2012 eel and frog spawn spots; the Lumbridge "Eel of Fortune" deed now counts a Raw cave eel.
- Silverlight and quest fights (9a2b4fc2, 9d03bf68, f244471b): every equippable quest reward is wearable (equipment count 994 to 1049); quest fights take ordinary ticks per review; Delrith needs a wielded Silverlight; the HUD names the boss.
- Barcrawl steps (a953836b): ten automatic stages at 2012 prices (208 coins); the shop drink rows and proofs are gone; quest-blocked dialogs list one reason per line.
- Belt tools (7bca9f86): the 2012 tool belt is one constant and one predicate for quests, Farming and Vinesweeper; ten bare-ground spade digs and the period exceptions stay held; the Bank Toolbelt panel lists the belt.
- Shop locks and purchase (1d401116, 5e3000e4): locked shops sort last, dim and name their reason; a purchase chip under Buy, green for success, amber for a refusal.
- Skill target search (eda7c2ed, 06fa2da4): every Skills-tab target list has the Combat search bar through `ui/ui_target_search.py`.

Merge repair (42adb030): eight module-surface digests, the root Qt digest and the equipment count re-pinned on the merged tree; the locked-shop wrap test fixed to a 240 px list because the test host's font fit the longest label (`382 not greater than 392`). First merged gate on a22202c6 failed only on those pins, that test, and three video status pages (fixed on main at 6637e281). Second gate on 231cdd1a with `--remote-lanes`: plain `Ran 5097 tests in 471.671s`, `OK (skipped=558)`; offscreen Qt `Ran 5097 tests in 686.769s`, `OK (skipped=5)`; documentation, test-quality, reachability, worktree, diff and papercut checks passed. Landed on main as 231cdd1a on 2026-10-01 01:30; the owner must restart Anki to load it.

Second level, not fixed: the combat chips line "Kills: N · Trains ..." clips at 192 px beside Bank run for aggressive and controlled styles on main today; Phoenix Lair still asks for Knife, Secateurs and Tinderbox in the bank; the flat target list clips long locked-row labels behind a horizontal scrollbar; skill guide rows dim only by level, not by quest lock; Count Draynor's garlic, Spiked gauntlets price, Do No Evil and Fremennik Isles purchase proofs, 90 of 293 quest encounters without stats.

## Carried-overlay forced-restock cleanup — 2026-10-02

Branch: `goal/carried-overlay-sixth-2026-10-02`; slice commit `d99ee5cb`; base main `60d7bd835c77b08f7040c67c77db4da7a233e648`.
Worktree: `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/ankiscape_worktrees/goal-carried-overlay-sixth-2026-10-02`.
Evidence: [retained raw output](</Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-carried-overlay-sixth-2026-10-02/>). Nothing is copied into the repository.

The dispatcher delegates forced banking to `_bank_trip_forced`.
Its triggers are a manual request, a refillable processing shortage, or an Agility pack item the bank can restock.
The helper preserves short-circuit order and projects the Agility pack even on a manual request.
Actions, trips, inventory, messages, saves, and RNG behavior stay unchanged.
The review fixes change documentation and that helper's docstring only.

Historical slice metrics (CCN limit 15, cognitive limit 15, Halstead difficulty limit 80):

| Version / function | CCN | Cognitive | Halstead difficulty | NLOC |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Before / `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` | 66 | 66 | 8.25 | 189 |
| After / `run_answer_with_carried_overlay` | 57 | 61 | 8.038461538461538 | 173 |
| After / `_bank_trip_forced` | 10 | 5 | 3.0 | 21 |

The metric instruments exit 1 because the existing dispatcher exceeds the limits; the helper passes.
The slice grew the module from 968 to 977 lines, below the 1,000-line ratchet; the review docstring adds two lines.

Differential proof uses the frozen base overlay, copied inputs, and identical RNG seeds.
It compares return/exception, final state and dictionary order, callback arguments and snapshots, messages, saves, and RNG state.
It also compares directed forced-trip decisions and repack calls.
Counts: 12056 cases, comprising 56 directed and 12000 generated; 0 differences.
Outcomes: 11880 normal returns and 176 identical exceptions; all directed cases returned normally.
Directed coverage includes manual banking, processing shortages, Thread, belt tools, potion presence, Agility restocking, Keep/Drop, cadence, and Winter Storage rollback.

## Remote mirror gate priority — 2026-10-02

Incident: gate `cfa311366d5e59e84e5d1f5856a363d402b0e639`, `Fri Oct  2 18:32:44 2026`, logged `TIMEOUT: waited 3600 seconds for /Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Caches/ankiscape-remote-test-host.lock` in `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/ankiscape_worktrees/gate-cfa31136.log`; raw copy `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-gate-mirror-priority-2026-10-02/original-gate.log`.

Tool-user gain: a waiting promotion gate stops new runners at a sibling turnstile instead of competing against an unbounded stream of kernel-queued mirror acquisitions. Runners already past admission finish; the gate keeps its heartbeat, one 3600 s wait bound across both locks and inherited mirror descriptor for both lanes. POSIX record locking covers admission; mirror flock behavior and existing tests stay unchanged.
New behavior tests use real processes and locks, pipe-controlled gate polling and generously bounded event waits: acquisition ordering against three arriving runners, and timeout while admission is occupied. No sleep-tuned timing assertions.

## Remote mirror kernel wait: 2026-10-02

Older runners bypass the turnstile and wait in `flock`. The gate now waits through a worker with `fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX)`. The main thread keeps the heartbeat and the shared 3600 s bound. The gate also checks the deadline before its first mirror acquisition.

Timeout closes the original descriptor and the turnstile before the caller receives `None`. The worker always closes its duplicate when `flock` returns. This releases any late acquisition. The gate keeps the turnstile and the descriptor that its lane runners inherit.

Two new tests use real processes and locks. One limits old-runner holds before the gate to three among six queued runners. The other forces the 3600 s bound and proves a late acquisition releases while the gate process stays alive. Pipe events control order. The existing fixture waits for worker completion before it resumes the gate.

## Remote mirror priority review fixes — 2026-10-02 to 2026-10-03

The gate keeps its blocking thread. The only production paths are `promotion_gate.py run -> run_gate -> _run_gate_checks -> _hold_remote_mirror` and the one-shot verification utility `tools/maintenance/verify_remote_mirror_priority.py main`. A mirror timeout makes `run` finish the remaining audits and exit with an error; the utility exits with status 1. `promotion_gate.py land` does not acquire the mirror. No production caller retries or serves more work after a timeout. The function docstring names these callers and states that the daemon worker and its duplicate can remain blocked until process exit or holder release.
The real-process fixture reports `worker-started` after the queue-call event. The test waits for that event and `poll` before it expires or resumes the gate. A retry waits for the worker-start event and joins the known worker without the former optional branch. The turnstile test requires each new runner's first event to be `waiting-turnstile` and checks the notice before it releases the current mirror holder; the overtaker branch is gone.
The old-runner test retains its limit of three holds among six queued runners. Its comment states the macOS scheduling assumption: the queue-call event does not prove kernel enqueue order. The updated runner tries nonblocking admission first and reports the turnstile path before a blocking wait.
The verification utility holds the mirror across fresh local and remote inventories, five consecutive focused proofs with `--expected-tests`, and the full plain lane. It writes a pending checkpoint before each command, then records the exit status and raw output. All five focused proofs and the full plain lane passed.

## Main-menu loot preference cleanup, 2026-10-02

The menu keeps the same behavior. A maintainer can change gathering Keep/Drop and combat loot preferences in `core/menu_loot.py` without reading the full menu closure. The root menu binds the live add-on module with the same `functools.partial` pattern as `core/menu_cooking.py`; callbacks resolve the current profile and skill on every call.

## Equipment source-digest review fixes — 2026-10-02

The catalog reproduction test now uses its temporary JSON digest manifest. The refusal test seeds sentinel output bytes and checks that both output and manifest survive a refused source change. The source-replacement test seeds only a foreign manifest entry instead of the real repository manifest.

Restored the weapon-type docstring that explains combat attack-style selection. The deleted `_read` wrapper is obsolete after parsing captured bytes; output-directory path insertion is redundant because the add-on root and package alias already resolve repository imports. The generator has no net line growth. The gitignored lock file remains in place. No UI, Magic or Dream changes.

## Worktree rules review fixes — 2026-10-02 to 2026-10-03

Tool gain: add and lease publish a complete staged rule set after output containment checks. Local Rulesync overrides refuse generation. Each Rulesync subprocess has a 60-second timeout. Failed add removes only its newly created destination; failed lease returns its lease. No gameplay or UI change.

All six review findings are addressed: the offscreen screenshot source contains the canonical body verbatim; output and parent escapes refuse before publication; timeout cleanup returns the lease; failed add can retry; a content-hash manifest removes obsolete untracked sidecars only when current bytes match previously generated bytes; symlinks in output paths or their worktree parents refuse before any deletion or publication; navigation has the full metrics command, positive facts, a bullet repository map and the mirror-wait guidance. Sidecars without a recorded content hash, or with edited bytes, remain unowned; cleanup does not infer ownership from an ignored filename.

Second level, not fixed: fresh worktrees receive rules only; skills are not generated. This slice created or deleted no worktree registered to the live repository. The rules-generation fixtures mock all add/remove and pool operations; the existing promotion tests use disposable repositories. The canonical checkout was read only, except for evidence files in its allowed Git evidence directory.

Evidence root: `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/Library/Application Support/Anki2/addons21/ankiscape_fork/.git/ankiscape-promotion-evidence/goal-worktree-rules-generation-2026-10-02/`.

- Rule comparison: a scratch copy ran `/Users/ArmanTarkhanian1/.local/bin/rulesync generate --config rulesync.jsonc --features rules` → `🎉 All done! Written 6 file(s) total (6 rules)`; `review-scratch-generate.log`. Every canonical root and `.claude/rules/*.md` file has a generated counterpart. Both canonical Qt sidecars are byte-identical. ## 2026-10-03: Combat bank-run decision cleanup
Enemy-specific supplies now have one helper; food, shared supply/loot capacity, ammunition and rune decisions keep their order and results. No gameplay, UI, save or message change.
